Pipeline: load sequence → compute MFE structure → calculate partition function and base pair probability matrix → visualize dot-bracket notation → assess RNA-RNA duplex formation. Use for siRNA/sgRNA targeting, ribozyme design, and RNA accessibility analysis. Use mfold or RNAfold CLI directly for batch command-line use without Python. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/viennarna-structure-prediction) · ★1)

- **vigen**: Make talking-avatar videos with the Vigen API (vigen.io). Sets up "koi" (an avatar from a photo plus a voice cloned from an audio clip, saved in the project's vigen-koi/ folder) and turns text or audio into lipsync videos with them, splitting long scripts into ~20 s parts joined into one video (with the seam times), with gestures Claude plans phrase by phrase and times to the words. Use when the user mentions Vigen, koi, a talking avatar, lipsync videos, cloning a voice, or Vigen TTS. ([vigen-io/vigen-plugin](https://github.com/vigen-io/vigen-plugin/tree/HEAD/vigen/skills/vigen))
- **viggle-ai**: Tired of static images and lifeless character art that never moves the way you imagine? Viggle-ai solves that by letting you animate any character, blend them into real video scenes, and generate expressive motion-driven clips — all without a film crew or animation studio. Whether you want to make a meme dance, drop a fictional character into a live-action clip, or create original short-form content, viggle-ai handles the heavy lifting. Built for creators, social media enthusiasts, and digital artists who want cinematic results fast. ([MacD1998/SkillSecurer](https://github.com/MacD1998/SkillSecurer/tree/HEAD/dataset/SkillTrustBench/secureSkills/case_00402) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **viggle-motion-transfer-comfyui**: Transfer motion from any video onto a character image with the NeuroVigal or Viggle Remake model in ComfyUI on 6GB VRAM: INT8 ConvRot model, Qwen VL auto-prompting, 6 Euler steps at 480p or 720p, RelayVSR 2x upscale, up to 12 seconds with source audio preserved. Trigger when a character needs to perform a motion from a reference video without motion capture. ([Emperiusm/skill_library](https://github.com/Emperiusm/skill_library/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-video/viggle-motion-transfer-comfyui) · ★1)
- **viggle-remake**: Viggle Remake in Comfiest — H3 LoRA pair for recasting and editing clips, one to four painted frames, source motion/camera/audio preserved, 480p on 24 GB. ([BuffMcBigHuge/Comfiest](https://github.com/BuffMcBigHuge/Comfiest/tree/HEAD/skills/viggle-remake) · ★9)
- **vigia**: Use when you need to audit a website or a local build for responsiveness, cross-browser rendering (including WebKit/Safari), broken layout, smoke errors (JS exceptions, failed assets, broken images), HTTP delivery config (compression, caching, security headers, redirects), and performance (Lighthouse budget). vigia diagnoses and never edits code. Trigger when the user asks "is my site broken / responsive / fast / working on Safari", "audit this site", "why won't it load", "check my deploy", or before shipping a frontend change. ([luizporto-ai/vigia](https://github.com/luizporto-ai/vigia/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **vigia**: O que fazer ao receber um alerta ou bloqueio do monitor do modo-deus (mensagens "[modo-deus · V1]" a "[modo-deus · V11]"). Use quando um hook devolver alerta de fuga de escopo, alucinação de arquivo ou comando, entrega sem prova, loop, estouro de teto ou teste moldado ao código, e quando o Maestro precisar decidir o que fazer com esses alertas. - **vigia**: Revisa con criterio lo que detectó el vigía de actualizaciones: versiones nuevas de las herramientas externas que tenés instaladas (plugins, skills, MCPs, CLIs). Clasifica cada novedad y la audita. Si una actualización pasa la auditoría y no rompe nada, la aplica y lo informa. Pregunta solo por lo que rompe, lo nuevo o lo riesgoso. Usala cuando el usuario diga 'revisá las novedades del vigía', 'revisalas', 'qué hay nuevo', 'qué avisó el vigía', 'actualizá las herramientas', o cuando al abrir la sesión aparezca 'Vigía: N novedades', 'Vigía caído', 'Vigía sin una búsqueda completa', 'no se pudo… ([raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo](https://github.com/raimondifernando-web/claude-catalogo/tree/HEAD/plugins/metodo/skills/vigia))

Use quando a CI de um PR ficar vermelha. Não corrige nada.
